## 2026-10-22 independent-observer attempt — current evidence disposition

Candidate identity was checked before collection: `HEAD` and product identity are
`7a1fb758a87b0057fb8622bd8ff3aa662e4db363`.  Its only change from the
authenticated installed `7ad8d990151540d2f9dc5e97a74931e41a19cf65` is this
evidence document, so the installed package named below exercises the same product
bytes.  The normal isolated installation remains
`delivery-7ad8d990/install/node_modules/@fromstan/pokie`, package
`@fromstan/pokie@2.0.0`, CLI entry SHA-256
`94e75699b0298f71a100f5233837c8889ae944345b1e04250c1c8f1aa532b30d`.

A new, separately provisioned observer used the initial-observer launcher, the
neutral 14-row public charter, public rendered Studio controls and an isolated
writable copy of `Valera35FeralBlazingSafari.blueprint.json`.  The observer recorded
the complete coverage plan before product actions and did not receive this document,
reviewer feedback, candidate source or expected labels.  Its actual chronology is
`/home/stager/Work/sta-ger/agents/runtime/verifier-harnesses/V2C-35-b12dcdbdca0fa31c/run-2026-10-22T20-39-11-594Z/transcript.json`
(`sha256:980ad20d72634465009a494059f4d65f841b3fd1b3c043d1b381531e5459bc45`);
the two retained images are `1-observation.png`
(`sha256:5f93444476c44534b9628953d0f94852794369bcda5ff6d9fb60833ffb32649e`)
and `2-observation.png`
(`sha256:c924bd3a084fb577401928e8701deb94bd021fb1338499514c5b2d2deeca1877`).
They were independently inspected: the first shows the installed Overview/public
navigation and fixture identity; the second shows the narrow Game Model surface.

The attempt reached Overview, Game Model, the Reels editor, Symbol weights and an
800×600 viewport.  At observations 25–114, clicking the rendered `Play` control
from the edited Game Model was accepted as `project-tab:gameModel` navigation but
only rendered `Preparing the selected section…` with lifecycle terminal `loading`;
the bounded wait expired without `Play ready`, an error, or another local terminal
state.  Later observations 120 and 132 still show that same loading lifecycle.  The
observer did not resend that accepted action.  The Studio child emitted only its
normal listening line, and owned processes had drained at close.

This is a **readiness-inconclusive**, not a product finding: the blind attempt did
not reach a successful freeze, the action has no rendered failure/terminal state,
and the source-backed-stall exception is unavailable without frozen initial
observations.  The observer therefore retained `collection_stop.reason=prerequisite`
and did not relabel its partial activity as a complete journey.  It also means the
remaining weighted persistence, extraction, optimizer Apply, long-job,
cancel/fail/retry/reload/reattach, destination-conflict, stale-action, focus and
small-viewport rows remain uncollected.  No historical result is promoted to close
them.  The next verifier-owned action is to repair the stalled/ambiguous public
navigation collection path and continue the same literal journey with a new
unexposed observer before any acceptance disposition.

## Source-discovered corrections

These are implementation findings against the source base, not observations by an
independent persona. Severity is provisional material P2 pending independent review.

| ID | Actual reproduction and impact | Production correction | Named regression and recheck status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| V35-S1 | Render the real `JobProgressCard` with `status: running` and no progress sample. Title says Running, detail says Queued, potentially implying lost or stalled work. New running case fails on the base implementation. | Missing-stage detail uses the lifecycle label; running still explains indeterminate progress, omits invented percentage/rate and retains Cancel. | `JobProgressCard.test.tsx`: `describes a running job honestly before its first progress sample`, plus queued sibling and exact Cancel dispatch. Independent installed affected recheck pending. |
| V35-S2 | After two advancing samples, keep the same progress object and change job id, project id or execution start. The old rate remains. Likewise pending/cancelling status retains an ETA derived from execution work rather than cleanup. Five new parameterized cases fail on the base implementation. | Rate baselines belong to job/project/start identity and running status. Pending cancellation clears rate and baseline; rejected cancellation needs fresh advancing observations before another estimate. | `JobProgressCard.test.tsx`: changed execution `id`/`projectId`/`startedAt`; cancellation `pending`/`cancelling`, including return to running. Independent installed affected recheck pending. |

Focused development reproduction, before production edits:

```text
npm run test:targeted -- tests/cli/studio-client/src/components/common/JobProgressCard.test.tsx
FAIL tests/cli/studio-client/src/components/common/JobProgressCard.test.tsx
Test Suites: 1 failed; Tests: 6 failed, 14 passed, 20 total; Time: 2.596s
POKIE_TARGETED_TEST_RESULT: pid=3924152 exitCode=1 signal=null
Exit code: 1
```

The changed shared component has three public consumers: Home and Project retained
jobs through `JobCard`, and Build/Export's Outcome Library generation directly.
Home/Project lists key cards by job id; the direct generation slot can survive a
changed execution. All receive the same status/stage correction. `JobCard` focus
ownership and active-to-terminal continuity remain covered in its existing cases
in the same test file. Rates are presentation-only: no executor, cancellation,
source authority, request, retry, publication or server lifecycle was changed.
CLI and direct-library behavior therefore consume no changed runtime dependency.

`tests/v2/Valera35Regression.test.ts` additionally carries two focused source-aware
editor-to-filesystem safeguards: rejected JSON retains draft/last applied model;
conflicting weighted authorities reject Save while retaining source bytes, corrected
numeric values/variant identity persist only through explicit Save; stale Save
preserves the external version and local draft, followed by explicit Save As to a
new path. The latter covers the authoritative `StudioBlueprintService` boundary,
not a mocked successful UI response. These safeguards do not claim a new independent
finding, browser recovery result or coverage of the complete charter.

`npm run build-studio-client`, inherited by CLI/build/prepack. Its build, installed
rendered behavior, included help, packaging and independent affected recheck are
controller-owned and pending. Typecheck/component tests cannot prove those bytes.

The scope preflight's existing tests are a coverage map, not an instruction to run
the entire list. Only the affected component file and required new regression are
selected here. Navigation workflow, integration, packaging, independent acceptance,
pre-review sanity, targeted/changed-test gates and normal integration/internal
publication remain controller-owned. No forbidden suite, process monitor or
background work has been launched.

Production/test changes are committed before the final bounded run. Planned commands:

```text
npm run test:targeted -- tests/v2/Valera35Regression.test.ts tests/cli/studio-client/src/components/common/JobProgressCard.test.tsx
npm run typecheck
```

Exact committed command results will be appended after execution. Until then these
commands are planned, not passed. No material finding is closed by this handoff;
the mandatory V2C-35-ACCEPTANCE public workflow and independent review must decide

## V35 optimizer terminal correction — implementation handoff

Correction base: `d4124219` (2026-10-22). The preceding report, literal checklist,
and original observations remain unchanged. This addendum owns the product repair;
it does not supply independent collection or acceptance.

Read-only inspection authenticated the retained transcript and screenshot above:

```text
transcript.json sha256:d46087e8f1c149082a10659aa6f008f9abfef929c831bd35c221380a42c7e440
2-observation.png sha256:1d7ffb7769ec01499ec5244d747997caef922c96c3cdf70fee6f4d58668341fc
```

Observation 45 at `09:43:45.067Z` actually records `running: 3 / 100 candidates
Finite search`, enabled `Cancel optimization`, disabled policy input, and the
same page's `optimization · Completed` / `Optimization infeasible` card. The raw
screenshot observation 46 at `09:43:58.302Z` shows the panel subsequently settled:
`completed: indeterminate / indeterminate work Finalizing`, `Verdict: infeasible`,
and no Cancel button. Thus the earlier prose saying that screenshot preserves
the same contradiction is inaccurate; observation 45 still demonstrates a
material inconsistent transition. Later convergence does not waive that finding.

Source tracing at the correction base: `ProjectDashboardPage.commonJobs` owns
operation cards, while `OptimizationPanel` mounts another `useProjectJobs` /
`useDurableJobs` observer. Their separate list/detail requests, 500ms polling loops,
and retained snapshots can render different statuses for the same execution.
The optimization service already maps infeasible results to a completed durable
record; changing its verdict or timing would not correct the duplicate UI state.

The dashboard now supplies its existing observer to the panel as a required
property. All runtime, native outcome and artifact dashboards use this single
mount. Progress, terminal results, cancellation/pending errors, discovery refresh
after Start and project scoping consequently share the operation-card state.
The Start response remains a temporary fallback until discovery accepts that id;
the observed record takes precedence. No server job or optimizer semantics changed.

Parameterized completed/infeasible, failed, cancelled and recovery-required paths plus a new execution. | Independent affected recheck on corrected installed/rendered bytes in fresh state remains pending. |
| V35-S1 / V35-S2 | Existing JobProgressCard production/test bytes preserved; final bounded run includes that regression file. | Existing independent fresh-state rechecks remain pending. |
| Immutable criteria 1, 2, 4 and remaining reviewer checklist | Original evidence and literal classifications preserved; source-aware regression is not relabelled blind collection. No evidence or completed-step artifact was modified. | Controller owns complete initial charter, authentication, frozen evidence, recovery/navigation/keyboard/viewport journeys and acceptance. |
| Immutable criterion 3: every material finding fixed, regressed and independently rechecked | Product correction and focused regression supplied for the sole accumulated P1; prior source corrections retained. | No finding is independently closed or self-waived by this handoff. |

### Field-authority ledger and proposed ownership

| Retained value / presence | Independent authority / shared validation owner |
| --- | --- |
| Blueprint, including omitted paylines/wilds/scatters and authored zero/missing awards | GameBlueprintValidator and resolveSharedSlotMathModel; default resolution stays unchanged. No cache identity proves validity. |

| Evaluated screen, bet, payout and nested components | SharedSlotMath.evaluate validates screen/stake; existing Wasm kernel executes; readSharedSlotMathResult checks component shape, positions and total. No alternate evaluator or changed arithmetic. |
| Per-session results, credits, feature grants, RNG/generator and restoration | Existing VideoSlotSession / VideoSlotWithFreeGamesSession and validateExecutableState retain ownership; only stateless Wasm scratch memory may be shared. | Candidate revisions, selected tunables, request and optional executable provenance | Existing ProjectOptimizationService receipts, native readback, assertOnlyTuned, runtime metrics and apply revalidation. No payload/receipt schema or transition authority changes. |
| Durable status, cancellation and terminal UI | Studio job service and the already-corrected single dashboard observer. This correction must preserve the prior lifecycle fix; component checks remain required. |

The actual request is the retained exact-finite request already regressed above.
Trace: OptimizationPanel -> optimization/start -> StudioOptimizationService ->
ProjectOptimizationService -> measureGame -> generateExactWeightedOutcomeLibrary
-> streamExactWeightedOutcomesInternal. Raw accumulation yields to Node timers;
artifact construction over all unique grids only yields async-generator promises
(microtasks). There is no host-event-loop boundary or cancellation check in that
phase. Thus one candidate can starve the public jobs list/detail/cancel routes
past the client's 10-second deadline. Increasing that deadline or clearing the
notice would preserve the cause. The previous allocation fix bounds Wasm memory;
it does not prove HTTP responsiveness during artifact execution.

The failed assertion exposed an incorrect **test assumption**, not weight
normalization in the server. Source inspection of StudioBlueprintService.save,
serializeGameBlueprint, BlueprintEditorPage.runSave and GameModelTab.handleSave
shows two separate contracts: advanced Save writes a lossless JSON draft, while
the shared validate endpoint and required-valid Project Save gate semantic
validity. Save accepts unknown/unfinished authored content; it writes -1 verbatim
and load preserves it. Adding blanket validation to this writer would change
supported draft persistence. The corrected regression therefore carries the same
-1 through GameBlueprintValidator, Studio validate, explicit draft Save As and
reload, checks the same field diagnostic/no coercion, then repairs and repeatedly
saves valid input using the same service. No persistence/validator implementation
was changed to accommodate the test. This refines the pre-edit ledger's Save row:
serialization/persistence is byte authority, not semantic validity authority.

The shared editor's positive status is also shortened to “A weight added to the
draft.” Home's Create game and Project's Save retain their own persistence
instructions; the shared field must not prescribe a nonexistent Home Save button.
Only that text, its rendered assertion, this failed test and this receipt change
after `6b160a18`. Generator, adapter, runtime, panel and progress executable/test
bytes remain identical, so the seven unaffected passing files are retained rather than
repeating the complete matrix. The failed step file and affected editor file,
plus root typecheck, must pass on the follow-up committed tree before handoff.
Independent installed acceptance, including rendered HTTP reattachment after the
scheduling correction, remains pending even though the actual executor/timer and
persisted lifecycle checks passed.

### Retry reflow and handoff lifetime — pre-edit transition audit

Correction base: `460485a70f2055c76dd05ce2e6443cdd1d146275`. The preceding
goal turn made progress (committed correction and bounded receipts), but the
reviewer's two remaining product findings disprove complete closure. Read the
retained `run-2026-10-22T18-09-20-238Z/observations.jsonl` and `transcript.json`
at the exact previously recorded evidence root. No collection, evidence mutation
or identical diagnostic rerun is needed. JSONL records 140/141 retain Retry at
`293,836.828125,66.859375,30` in `1439x956`; record 143 retains that same focused
button at `33,1049.390625,66.859375,30` in `390x844`. Collector decision 142
requests the resize. These raw record indices supplement the reviewer's
observation numbering without rewriting any original row or classification.

The unsuccessful strategy was preserving only the restored **region** through
layout changes, then treating every retained request reference as a fresh
Configure instruction on mount. Keeping a region's leading edge visible does
not keep its lower Retry button visible. Replaying a request after it has been
submitted or superseded by report selection makes navigation an unintended
state transition. The repair must bind scrolling to the actual DOM focus owner
and bound simulation handoff lifetime to explicit Run/report transitions.

| Field / transition | Authority and existing validation owner | Repair / compatibility obligation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Focused descendant, region, sibling job, dialog | Connected `document.activeElement` inside this JobCard; browser geometry and AppShell header bounds. | Scroll the actual owner on ancestor layout and viewport resize; never refocus a live sibling/dialog. Preserve disconnected-control -> region restoration and release observers/listeners/frames on unmount. |
| Explicit Retry activation | JobResultCard -> ProjectDashboardPage.handleJobRecoveryAction -> captured request copied per activation. | Same job may be selected repeatedly. A pending handoff is configuration authority only until explicit Run or report selection supersedes it; ordinary navigation must not recreate that authority. |
| rounds, seed, workers, modeName, convergence and omitted optional fields | Accepted durable `job.request`; validateSimulationRequest/validateSimulationConvergence and executable mode support remain semantic owners. SimulationTab rounds preflight preserves invalid drafts. | No new validator or inferred request. Retain clearing of absent fields, per-mode/all-mode budgets and local validation rejection; consume the handoff when an explicit valid submission starts, including an eventual server rejection. Its mounted form retains the attempted values for correction. |
| Hook Repeat/Retry, current execution, terminal receipt | useSimulationPoll's captured request, generation/currentJobId and source-revision guards. | This explicit execution also supersedes a pending handoff. Restore failed/cancelled Review on remount rather than forcing Configure from old state. No automatic POST. |
| Historical report id/detail and stale responses | selectReport's request sequence, report/compare invalidation and projectKey reset. | Report selection supersedes the simulation handoff even before its fetch resolves; old handoffs cannot override a selected report on revisit. |
| Project identity | Server-scoped jobs, projectKey reset and useSimulationPoll resetForProjectSwitch. | Preserve reset and response correlation; clear only simulation handoffs at simulation transitions, leaving other operation consumers intact. |

Shared callers audited before edits: JobCard has only HomePage (Design/import/
export/build and project-opening jobs) and ProjectDashboardPage (all retained
project operations). Both map stable job ids directly to this shared component;
neither needs a second visibility path. JobProgressCard/JobResultCard are its

The dashboard's same recovery dispatcher also forwards replay, certification,
Build/Export, play/new-session and project-opening actions; these retain their
existing requests and submission behavior. No subclasses/inheritors exist for
these function components. Explicit Configure Run, Recent Runs Run again and
feature Repeat/Retry are the simulation execution entry points; report opening
and completed-job auto-selection share selectReport. Automatic restart recovery
may prepare a configuration before submission; it must not re-arm a consumed
handoff. Home uses its own navigation-state recovery contract unchanged.

Stateless projections, API transport, durable request validators, service/library/
CLI executors, optimizer Apply and artifact publication owners are unchanged.
No accepted payload or executable snapshot validation boundary changes here.
Coordinated payload tampering/generation observations and same-instance snapshot
restoration are therefore inapplicable to these two UI ownership defects. The

existing owning-validator and public-caller regressions; no fallback/coercion or
parallel validation path is introduced. Transition regressions will cover
explicit selection -> repeated selection -> explicit replacement -> failed/
cancelled terminal -> leave/return -> fresh selection, report selection/revisit,
and rejected submission -> corrected first/subsequent successful reuse.

Current material finding V2C-35-OPTIMIZER-PROGRESS (P2): observations 126–133
show a public `Check tuning support` success (`Supported finite same-type
workflow`), then an accepted `Run optimization` that rendered `optimization ·
Running` and `running: 0 / unknown work` at observations 129 and 130. The
observer cancelled the still-active operation once; its action-local terminal
was `Optimization cancelled`, `0 evaluated, 0 rejected, 1 consumed`, while its
diagnostic disclosed that weighted-outcome generation had completed 46,656 raw
draws. No reviewed candidate or Apply control was rendered. The user cannot see
meaningful work/progress during the required supported optimization, so the
successful reviewed Apply workflow remains blocked.

Post-freeze candidate-source tracing explains the observed state without
rewriting it: `cli/studio/optimization/StudioOptimizationService.ts` emits a
job-progress record only when `ProjectOptimizationService.optimize` calls its
`onProgress`; `src/optimization/ProjectOptimizationService.ts` calls that only
after candidate materialization and measurement. The initial generated
weighted-outcome work therefore has no progress callback, while
`OptimizationPanel.tsx` defaults its status display to `0 / unknown work`.
This is the root cause of the rendered opaque-running state, not a driver wait
threshold. It requires a product correction and a fresh independent affected
recheck; it is not closed by the observer's user-initiated cancellation.

The raw runtime record, four screenshots and fixture/output trees remain outside
the committed evidence payload. No product file, test, archive or installed
artifact was changed in this verification disposition.

Correction base: `8904d975ab20669f131df3adf423cfbdd698a504`. The retained
`run-2026-10-22T20-57-05-275Z/frozen-initial.json` and `transcript.json`

by opaque running work and user cancellation, not a completed candidate whose
Apply control was hidden. The existing committed Feral Blazing Safari fixture
is the retained 46,656-draw workload; its actual retained bytes are authoritative.

attempt before measurement, and cannot describe completed work inside that
attempt. Cancellation must still produce no partial candidate or Apply authority.

Pre-edit field/transition ledger:

| Field or transition | Executable/accepted authority and shared owner |
| --- | --- |
| request, targets, budget, maxOutcomes, optional sampling, scope/stake/mode | validateOptimizationRequest, adapter support/preflight and existing native/executable population owners; no progress field is accepted as a request. |
| sourceRevision, candidate revision/type/path, requestRevision and optional executableRevision | projectRevision, same-type resolver/native readback, assertOnlyTuned, package dependency authority and service-owned receipt; progress is not publication authority. |
| raw current/total | Existing generateExactWeightedOutcomeLibrary onProgress from the actual prepared reel-stop enumeration; retain decimal integer strings, without Number coercion or estimating unique-outcome total from raw draws. |
| emitted outcomes and optional total absence | Existing onPostEnumeration/onPostEnumerationProgress from actual artifact construction. Deduplication means total is unknown here; show an explicit indeterminate total, never the raw-draw total. |
| executable verification work | Existing verifyWasmExecutable execution loop and exact source generator; keep sampled validationWork accounting unchanged. |
| evaluated/rejected/consumedWork and phase | ProjectOptimizationService loop and runStochasticOptimization; attempts, completed candidates and population work are different units. Clear local work on the next candidate/completed measurement. |
| job id/status/cancellation and terminal identity | StudioJobService and its durable repository; StudioOptimizationService only projects execution observations. Shared Home/Project JobCard and OptimizationPanel consume the same job. Terminal status always owns cancellation eligibility. |
| Apply/save and refresh/consumption | Existing publish/apply receipt checks, validation of reopened executable, onAuthoredCommit/runtime refresh and durable authoredApply marker; telemetry cannot grant or reconstruct a receipt. |

exact loop -> acquireOptimizationAdapter -> native library/Stake or authored
Blueprint/PAR/declared TS/canonical WASM materialize/read/measure -> exact
generator and WASM proof. measure is also called by publish (and apply staging);

lost units at adapters and fabricated a durable denominator downstream.

Field-authority ledger, established before editing:

| Field | Authority / owner | Required projection |
| --- | --- | --- |
| status / cancellation / execution | builder lifecycle, StudioJobService execution and cleanup | Progress never authorizes completion, publication or cancellation completion. |
| generation completed / total | exact/sampled generator callback for a specific mode | Raw combinations/draws only; never records or final publication. |
| writing completed / absent total | streaming writer's persisted record loop | Outcome records; no inference from preflight. |
| analysis completed / total | writer's actual per-mode record count and analysis pass | Separate per-mode/pass phase and record unit. |
| indexing completed / total | native index copy's measured bytes | Separate per-mode phase and bytes. |
| published files completed / total | writer/exporter inventory and file loop | Files only; no source record denominator. |
| optional phase / unit / count presence | producer callback, ArtifactBuildProgress forwarding contract | Absent measurement remains indeterminate; legacy injected callbacks remain supported without invented units. |
| preflight estimates | generation request or inspected source manifest | Advisory only, retained separately from phase measurements. |
| percentage / rate / ETA | common card validates the current sample and consecutive same-execution/phase/unit/total observations | Reject malformed, unsafe numeric, negative and over-total samples; reset after invalid samples and cancellation. |

| --- | --- | --- |
| reels, rows, symbols, wilds, scatters, screenGeometry | Authored GameBlueprint; GameBlueprintValidator, validateBlueprintReelSequences and ScreenProjection; buildGameModelProjection supplies symbol roles and geometry. | Read current projected values; preserve circular stops and visible row offset. |

| variant ID, ordered symbols, numeric weight, zero-weight presence | WeightedSymbolsSequence owns unique IDs, equal lengths, known symbols, nonnegative binary64 weights and positive total; validator delegates to it. No probability calculator, RNG, execution selection or persisted field. |
| weightedVariants vs positions, analysis and sharedWeightsSample absence | buildGameModelReels copies authored alternatives without claiming a resolved strip; exact definitions keep literal positions. | Render the returned alternatives directly; do not fabricate a resolved reel or a sampled outcome library. |
| local selected ID and stop | Reader's inspection action only; current returned variant definition remains data authority. | Resolve against current props every render. Changed symbols/weights/IDs cannot revive cached strip content; removed/disabled selection falls back to the current first eligible alternative. |
| Save/hash and Apply authority | Existing WholeSequencesEditor validation/explicit Apply, GameModelTab validation and StudioBlueprintService.save conflict checks. | Inspection dispatches no mutation, Save or runtime request. |

### 2026-10-23 remediation packet — optimizer-source-revision

Scope: the controller-issued `optimizer-source-revision` packet on parent
`bba82d7e` (the commit containing this section is the implementation candidate;
its exact SHA and committed-tree command outcomes accompany the strict work
report). This disposition appends to the frozen record above. It does not close
V2C-35 or claim an installed independent affected recheck.

Finding trace: read the retained
`/home/stager/Work/sta-ger/agents/runtime/verifier-harnesses/V2C-35-6ade75b8df873465/run-2026-10-23T20-23-54-987Z/transcript.json`.
Observations 106, 111, 114 and 119 confirm the editable Paytable, explicit Save,
saved value and return to the obsolete Overview terminal. Source inspection on
the parent reproduces the ownership gap: GameModelTab calls the dashboard's Save
callback, which invalidates simulation and refreshes context, but OptimizationPanel
receives neither a source change nor a current revision and restores its terminal
from ordinary durable discovery.

Fix: successful Blueprint saves return the optimizer's authored-byte
`sourceRevision` separately from the Blueprint model-content `blueprintHash`.
The dashboard retains this project-scoped identity across tab unmounts and routes
both Game Model Save and authored optimizer Apply through source invalidation.
An accepted new optimization records its reviewed source identity at that same
owner, preserving current-result review after leaving and returning to Overview.
Optimizer request, evidence and candidate revisions must agree with the current
known source. An obsolete terminal is explicitly historical, retains inspectable
evidence, and exposes neither Apply nor Save reviewed candidate. Unavailable
identity after a successful write invalidates conservatively; renewed support can
establish the current revision. Saving a separate candidate does not invalidate
source or simulation. Active jobs retain cancellation and their durable observer;
late terminals are assessed against the saved source when they arrive.

| Packet acceptance | Implementation and focused regression disposition |
| --- | --- |
| Accepted Game Model Save makes older terminal stale and removes both actions | `V35 saved-source revision follows the accepted Game Model edit back to Overview and permits a fresh run` exercises the rendered editor, persisted fixture value, successful response, route transition, retained operation card and stale notice. `V35 source revision ownership` covers request-only, evidence-only, candidate-only, disagreement and missing identities. |
| Current-source review, cancellation, isolation, discovery and simulation remain intact | Matching-source test executes both Save-as and Apply. Fresh current terminal remains actionable after tab remount. Active old work remains cancellable and its late terminal becomes historical. Invalid, conflicting and cancelled saves preserve current optimizer review. Existing whole-file discovery/project-switch/lifecycle and saved-design simulation cases remain in the mandatory bounded run. |
| New optimization uses current source | Late old support is ignored after Save; renewed support submits the new source revision exactly once. The routed dashboard test follows accepted Save through current-source start and terminal review. |
| Preserve independent history and require affected recheck | No retained verification file was changed or recollected. Controller-owned installed recheck, independent review and official gates remain pending. |

### 2026-10-24 package dependency correction — implementation authority and closure ledger

Correction base: `0c0dd617` (the independent package-dependency finding above).
This is implementation work; the frozen finding, transcript, screenshot, delivery
receipt and earlier accepted observations remain unchanged. The correction candidate
and committed-tree check receipts will be recorded below after execution.

Retained bytes read: the named `delivery-db3ef623/receipt.json`, the complete
`run-2026-10-24T07-59-01-997Z/transcript.json`, its `1-observation.png`, and
`fixtures/descriptor-package/package.json` under the same retained harness root.
The latter confirms `graceful-serene-frontier`, seed 35, and the exact failing
dependency `@fromstan/pokie: ^2.0.0`. No retained script/command was executed and
no installed/browser/registry collection was performed by this worker.

Pre-edit source trace on the correction base: `readOwnPackageRoot` supplies the
running installation to `registerCliCommands` -> `BuildCommand` and
`StudioCommand` -> `StudioServer` -> `StudioArtifactBuildService`. Both artifact
consumers forward it through `ArtifactBuilderRegistry.withRuntimePackageRoot`
to `TsPackageArtifactBuilder`. That builder previously added only a symlink
after `GamePackageGenerator` published six files. `buildPackageJsonPatch` emitted
`^2.0.0`, and `renderBuiltPackageLock` repeated it. The CLI Next instruction and
generated README asked for `npm install`; the README incorrectly promised that
the version range made this unpublished candidate portable. A convenience link
does not make an unavailable registry dependency installable. This disproves
the earlier link-only strategy rather than reclassifying the finding.

| Field / optional presence | Model, executable or accepted-transition authority; shared owner |
| --- | --- |
| Runtime installation location | CLI entry's own `import.meta.url`, independently of the Blueprint or returned package metadata. The existing registry/builder composition forwards this trusted dependency. Paths are normalized at generation, not inferred from matching mutable copies. |
| Generated dependency spec, and matching lockfile root dependency | Existing `withLocalPokieDependency` writes `file:` from that supplied installation; `renderBuiltPackageLock` projects the resulting manifest. Equality of those projections is a consistency check, not runtime identity proof. |
| Omitted runtime location | Direct library generation retains the existing `buildPackageJsonPatch` registry range; it performs no installation and promises no registry availability. The per-call option cannot leak to a later call. |
| Immediate runtime link and created-file receipt | `GamePackageGenerator` creates the link to the same supplied installation inside its staging directory before atomic rename. The returned created-file list includes that link so the existing Studio rollback owner removes it and its empty parent directories on registration failure. |
| Manifest identity, optional model fields, executable configuration, reels/weights/awards | Unchanged `GameBlueprintValidator`, authored-mechanics/sequence validators, reel resolution/materialization and generated runtime/descriptor. Dependency selection cannot replace or validate this model. |
| New/empty destination, cancellation and registration | Existing prepared conversion/destination owners, generator staging and cancellation checks, builder cleanup, and Studio registration rollback. A cancelled final file callback cannot publish the package or its runtime link. |
| Extraction fields, evidence and stale publication | Existing `ProjectTargetResolver`, runtime loader, `PackageBlueprintExtraction`, conversion planner and prepared Studio extraction; no fallback model or second extractor was added. Tests consume the actual generated package through these owners. |

Shared-boundary audit: `GamePackageGenerator` has no subclasses. Its direct callers
are the package artifact builder, `BlueprintProjectMaterializer` and the sibling
`StudioBlueprintService.build` editor endpoint. Materialization keeps its existing
temporary dependency-closure install/restoration, cache and release behavior;

No constructor default or shared manifest helper was changed. The new per-call
option is forwarded by the artifact builder; StudioCommand also configures its
sibling editor builder with the same running root. Injected generators continue
to receive the `GamePackageGenerating` options contract. CLI package extraction,
Studio import preview/open and extraction, library load/descriptor reading,
simulation/dev/replay/serve and PAR-to-package conversion consume the same package
contract, bare imports and existing conversion planner. Other artifact targets,

The changed configuration holders do not retain execution or snapshot history.
Focused transitions cover successful publication -> real link failure -> first
and second successful reuse in the same builder, registration rejection -> two
successful reuses in the same editor service, new and caller-owned empty
destinations, final-file cancellation, occupied/source-alias rejection and
optional-root omission on subsequent generator calls. Existing suites retain

boundary. Changing both generated manifests is not proof of a valid runtime:

### Field and transition authority before editing

| Value / presence | Authority and validation owner | Required transition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Current focused element | Live `document.activeElement`, connected HTMLElement, contained in the currently visible Projects panel; existing useDesignFocusVisibility checks these at callback execution. A retained launcher reference or matching label cannot establish current ownership. | Cancel restores the launcher; viewport/ancestor movement scrolls that actual control only. Focus moved to another project, navigation or portal dialog supersedes it, including between scheduling and execution. |
| Bounds and viewport | Live getBoundingClientRect and window dimensions; existing helper rejects zero-size controls and accounts for AppShell header and 8px margins. CSS scroll margins govern the browser's scrollIntoView placement. | Both desktop-to-small reflow and reverse/header displacement, height-only resize, and delayed ancestor layout changes must use current geometry. jsdom proves dispatch/ownership, not browser geometry. |
| Panel visibility and lifetime | HomePage supplies isVisible while retaining both tab bodies; ProjectsPanel's existing visibility lifecycle remains authoritative. | Enable the existing helper only for visible Projects; disconnect/cancel on hide or unmount. Never focus an old launcher or scroll on behalf of a detached control. |
| Project identity, confirmation, removal request | Existing registry response, handleRemove closure and useConfirm accepted callback; no change to payload, optional fields, list validation, registry mutation or onConfirm/onCancel semantics. | Cancel and resize send no removal request. Reopening and explicitly confirming removes only the chosen registry entry through the existing API. |

implementation/defaults. Its existing consumers are guided BlueprintEditorPage
and ExportDeployTab; ProjectsPanel becomes its third consumer. It has no
inheritors, forwarding adapters, CLI or direct-library callers. Existing JobCard

region. useConfirm also serves bulk removal and other confirmation callers and
is unchanged. The Projects region includes search, filters, Open, individual and
bulk Remove, relocation and import controls, so all use the same current-focus
rule. Portalled confirmations and path pickers are outside that region.

There is no accepted serialized state or malformed business payload changed by
this correction. Coordinated mutation of copied requests, RNG restoration,
stateless semantic validators and executed-rejection state recovery therefore
remain covered by their earlier owning-boundary receipts; new validators or

### Pre-edit field and transition authority

| Retained value / optional presence | Authority / shared owner | Transition obligation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Discovery pending, loaded targets (including empty), failure | Completion of the current `listArtifactTargets` request in this mounted project; `StudioArtifactBuildService.listTargets` and registry planner own semantic support. An initial empty array is not a completed observation. | Delay -> loaded empty or targets; failure -> explicit artifact retry; obsolete project/unmounted response cannot settle the new discovery. Deployment discovery retains its separate owner. |
| Target and optional destination | Current target-specific input; blank/trimmed input retains the existing omitted `outDir` convention. `previewArtifact` forwards it; service destination resolution and planner own safety/conflict semantics. | Editing one input immediately revokes only its old approval. Rapid edits coalesce behind at most one executing preview per target; only the latest applicable request may approve. |
| Preview status, source binding, extraction fields/hash, plan, optional prepared operation | Server preview and existing `toArtifactPreviewRunView` projection; extraction/Stake prepared-operation stores bind canonical project, source graph and destination. Equality between UI copies is not publication proof. | Refresh/failure/navigation invalidates approval. Build consumes the latest preview only; authoritative execution still revalidates source, destination and graph. No browser reconstruction or substitute validator. |
| Active job id, cancellation intent, run version | Existing per-target synchronous active-build owner, server durable execution and terminal cleanup. | Destination stays locked from submission through cleanup; obsolete job reads cannot restore result actions. Keep extraction success/conflict and source/foreign-destination guards. |
| Published Outcome Library and Stake dependency | Existing generation terminal and `refreshArtifactPreviews` -> server-prepared Stake operation. | Publication revokes prior handles before a follow-on Build; latest queued refresh must supersede in-flight older planning. Keep the sibling real-service handoff regression. |

is not one. Applicable recovery sequences are ready -> edit -> rejection ->
corrected destination -> repeated valid preview, pending -> refresh -> failure
-> retry, queued edit -> unmount/project switch, and generation -> refreshed
Stake Build. Explicit RNG/snapshot restoration is inapplicable to this UI queue.

### Source trace and field/transition authority

Before editing, traced both breadcrumb and brand through `handleClose`, the
server `/api/projects/close` guard, `cancelActiveJobsForOldProject`, the route
unmounts and Home's existing source-only discovery. The earlier approach of
reusing Close to reach Home necessarily cancels the operation. Adding a card
alone cannot establish the required continuing-work transition. Conversely,
removing Close's cancellation would weaken its established cleanup contract.
The correction separates a visit to Home from an explicit Close.

| Retained value / optional presence | Authority and shared validation owner | Consumer / transition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Current project root, or its absence | Server `GET /api/context` owns the actual current project. The new Home reader uses that response, never registry labels, a copied request source, history state or inferred runtime identity. | Pending/error/empty/project discovery; Return uses the encoded returned root. No project mutation occurs while visiting Home. |
| Context response lifetime | Component effect lifetime and abort controller, with a 10-second request budget; old subscribers cannot attach after cleanup. | Late context response after unmount; retry after failure. Reattachment also rechecks the current context. |
| Job project/id/creation/status/progress/request and optional recovery/result/output metadata | Existing project job API and `StudioJobService` own the durable record. `listProjectJobs` checks returned root against the expected root. `useProjectJobs` and `useDurableJobs` retain scope, owner/list sequence, revisions and monotonic cancellation/terminal merging. | Same adapter on Home and Project. Root changes remount the Home observer; obsolete observations cannot repopulate the new scope. No retained payload is reconstructed or promoted to execution authority. |
| Progress counts, units, totals and estimates | Existing producer contracts and `JobProgressCard.measuredProgress`, execution/phase/unit/cancellation sampling owners. | Home uses the same card as Project. Unknown/malformed denominators retain existing indeterminate fallback; no invented count/ETA is added. |
| Cancel and checkpoint Resume | Existing project-scoped cancel/recover API; server `handleCommonJob` forwards cancellation to simulation/replay/artifact/outcome executors and validates resumable checkpoint ownership. | Rejected Cancel retains the job and permits retry; accepted cancellation stays pending through cleanup; only its durable terminal removes the control. Resume reuses the server checkpoint path. |
| Configuration Retry and output inspection | Existing Project feature handlers, captured-request validators, current source/destination/prepared-operation guards. | Home offers Return to project for these workflows; it does not advertise an unhandled Retry or automatically replay a captured request. Existing terminal downloads remain record-owned. |
| Navigation versus cleanup | Router-owned navigation plus existing GameModel and Home Design dirty blockers; explicit Close and `useConfirmedProjectOpen` retain server transition confirmation. | Stay retains the draft; Leave visits Home without cancellation. Opening a different project and Close still drain through their original guards. Returning to the current root reuses context without another Open. |
| Focus and visibility | Live focused element and layout/viewport geometry under unchanged `JobCard`; Home's existing destination-panel focus and Project panel focus own route entry. | Shared Home cards retain sibling ownership and restore the region on active-to-terminal replacement. No card steals focus from another control/dialog. |

Shared-boundary audit: `getContext` has two production callers, StudioLanding
and the new HomeProjectJobs. Its only change is an optional AbortSignal; the
ordinary launch call and diagnostics are unchanged and covered by apiClient's
existing cases plus a forwarding assertion. There are no inheritors. The
unchanged `useProjectJobs` adapter gains HomeProjectJobs as a consumer alongside
ProjectDashboardPage (which forwards it to OptimizationPanel/feature workflows).
Its shared `useDurableJobs` sibling is `useHomeSourceJobs`, still consumed by
HomePage for Design/project-opening records. Home suppresses duplicate
project-opening cards in its project panel; their existing source-scoped Home
Open/Reveal/recovery actions remain authoritative. No common observer default,
server route, executor, compatibility bridge, stateless semantic validator,
package writer or library resolver was changed.

Applicable transition regressions cover Project -> Home Projects -> Home Design
-> Home Projects -> same scoped Project; active -> rejected cancellation -> lost
observation -> reconnect -> accepted cancellation -> cleanup terminal; failed
context/list -> successful discovery; old-root delayed response -> new-root
accepted context/list; checkpoint -> explicit Resume; dirty Stay -> repeated
navigation -> explicit Leave. Existing named adapter/component cases retain
repeated rejection/reuse, project/source/generation isolation, malformed progress
fallback, simulation captured optional-field clearing, explicit Run and consumed
Retry/report ownership. No serialized snapshot format or executable semantic
payload is changed and this finding supplies no malformed business-input
reproducer. Coordinated mutation of dependent payload copies, RNG restore and
new semantic evaluators are inapplicable here; equality between copies is not
used as evidence. Browser-owned WASM Workers retain their existing page/owner
lifetime; visiting Home cannot transfer a Worker into a server executor.
